How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Traveling Zoo
Aliases: TZ, Travelling Zoo, Zoo
Categories: Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Events, Traveling Zoo, Pets
Traveling Zoo
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Traveling Zoo is an [[Events|Event]] during which [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] will visit the [[Hub Island|SkyBlock Hub]] to sell exclusive [[Pets]]. Oringo can be found for 3 in-game Days in the [[Village]]. The [[Events|Event]] takes place twice a year, in Early Summer 1-3 (in-game time) and in Early Winter 1-3. A Traveling Zoo event happens, in real-life time, every 2 days and 14 hours, lasting 1 hour each.
## Stock
### Stock Distribution
In each traveling zoo, Oringo will sell 3 of the 6 possible pets listed below.
- One of the pets will be [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] - this pet is assigned in a sequence shown in the table below.
- The other two pets are randomly assigned, with a random rarity ranging from [[Rarity|COMMON]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]].
Predicted Time when the pet is next sold as Legendary:
|Pet|Year|
|-|-|
|[[Lion Pet]]|Summer, Year 522|
|[[Monkey Pet]]|Winter, Year 522|
|[[Elephant Pet]]|Summer, Year 523|
|[[Giraffe Pet]]|Winter, Year 523|
|[[Blue Whale Pet]]|Summer, Year 524|
|[[Tiger Pet]]|Winter, Year 524|

## Interface
This is an example of the Traveling Zoo interface.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event Start (Player not in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]))|The Traveling Zoo is visiting the SkyBlock Hub! CLICK HERE to shop for exotic pets!|
|Event Start (Player in the Hub)|Oringo has brought his Traveling Zoo shop in town! Acquire exclusive pets! Visit Oringo at the hub plaza, in front of the Flower House!|
|Entering the Hub During the Event|Oringo brought exotic pets for sale at the hub plaza, in front of the Flower House!|
|Event Start ([[Stranded]] only (This content is only available on the Stranded game mode.))|Oringo has brought his Traveling Zoo shop to your island! Acquire exclusive pets by talking to him!|
## Trivia
- The Traveling Zoo was introduced in SkyBlock year 52. The first-ever event happened on February 26, 2020.
- Players can be informed about the pets, along with their rarities, in the upcoming Traveling Zoo event one hour early by having [[Oringo]] as an Abiphone contact and picking up when they call.
- When the Traveling Zoo was first introduced, there was a bug that caused a copy of the [[Event Stand]] to spawn on all players' [[Private Island|Private Islands]]. It appeared for everyone at the same coordinates. If the event was active when the player joined their island, it would spawn a stand with animal heads. If the player joined their island when the event wasn't active, it would spawn question mark heads instead of animal heads.
- On July 1, 2021, only 2 pets were available for purchase.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- If a [[Hub Island|Hub]] server reboots during the Traveling Zoo, Oringo disappears for the rest of the event. Switching Hubs with the [[Hub Selector]] can fix this problem.
  - When this happens, right-clicking another player that is close enough to Oringo's normal position allows the player to open Oringo's menu and buy the pets for the next Traveling Zoo, which will happen six SkyBlock months later.
  - The Event Stand has its normal look, calling Oringo with an Abiphone lists the pets for the next Traveling Zoo, and Oringo is not visible.
  - The game message and scoreboard countdown still appear.
  - For example, on February 17, 2023, a patch was released about two minutes prior to the start of the event, and servers that didn't finish restarting until after the event had started were bugged as described above. In this case, the bugged lobbies had the pets for the February 20 event.
    - In islands other than Hubs, calling Oringo with an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] listed the correct pets.
    - In Hub servers that weren't affected by the bug, everything was normal: Oringo appeared as usual, calling Oringo with an Abiphone listed the correct pets, and the [[Event Stand]] was decorated for the Traveling Zoo.
    - On February 20, 2023, all Hub servers again had the correct pets. In other words, it was possible to see the same selection of pets at both events.
    - This happened again on May 6, 2023; May 16, 2023; and July 7, 2023.
## Gallery
- The Traveling Zoo in the Event Square

|Nov 8, 2021|Changed the game messages' description of the event's location from "...near the Colosseum!" to "...at the hub plaza, in front of the Flower House!".|

# Vanilla Objects
Aliases: Acacia Wood Slab, Armor Stand, Birch Wood Slab, Bottle, Dark Oak Wood Slab, Empty Bottle, Firework, Gold Apple, Jungle Wood Slab, Leash, Mushroom Stew, Name Tag, Oak Wood Slab, Painting, Spruce Wood Slab, Stair, Vanilla, Vanilla Item, Vanilla Items, Vanilla Mobs, Vanilla Object, Vanilla items, Vine, Wood Stair, Wood Stairs, Wooden Stair
Categories: Pages with UIs, Items, Vanilla objects
Vanilla Objects are items/mobs/blocks in vanilla (unmodified) Minecraft, and are in the game, but have little to no special purpose specific to SkyBlock, and as such are listed here to avoid page clutter. If vanilla items have an important enough SkyBlock-specific purpose a page should be made for them instead. Please visit the link to the Minecraft wiki's page on it for more details.

## Trivia
- [[Cooked Mutton]] was originally obtained using a Bazaar in a glitched lobby that allowed you to drop items from the Bazaar GUI. When [[Enchanted Cooked Mutton]] was dropped, the game automatically converted it into its glitched unenchanted form.
- Food items are mentioned in [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] meeting #5 when [[Cult of the Fallen Star|Brarnas]] states "Thondin made the realization that food actually has no taste or texture, though we are inclined to eat it". This statement is used by the cult members as evidence that the NPC's are living in a simulation.

# Wardrobe
Aliases: Armor Wardrobe
Categories: Pages with UIs, Armor, SkyBlock Menu
[Wardrobe]
Type: Storage
The Wardrobe is a storage system for [[Armor]], allowing the player to easily design setups and quickly swap between them.
Wardrobe is also used as a part of [[Loadouts]] system.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Usage
Players can store [[Armor]] in the Wardrobe. Entire [[Armor|armor sets]] can be added, or a mix of pieces can be entered, depending on which the player prefers. Wearing a full armor set for the first time will save the date in the armor's slot in the Wardrobe. The player cannot modify their current set.
The player can find the Wardrobe in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] after unlocking [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 5]] or using the /wd command to open the Wardrobe.
More armor slots are unlocked the higher the player's [[Ranks|Rank]].
|Rank|Slots|
|-|-|
|1Default ([[Ranks]])|4|
|2[VIP] ([[Ranks]])|6|
|3[VIP+] ([[Ranks]])|10|
|4[MVP] ([[Ranks]])|14|
|5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|18|
Wardrobe slots can also be upgraded from the [[Community Center]]
|Slot|Price|Cumulative Price|
|-|-|-|
|1|Free|Free|
|2|250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|3|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|750 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|4|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|1,250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|5|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|1,750 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|6|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|2,250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|7|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|2,750 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|8|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|3,250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|9|500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|3,750 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Trivia
- When the Wardrobe is disabled, the icon changes to undyed [[Chestplates|Leather Armor]] to signify that it is disabled.
- If a player's rank is downgraded by Hypixel Support, armor in newly locked Wardrobe slots automatically gets transferred to the player's [[Stash]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added Wardrobe.|
|May 16, 2021|Temporarily disabled Wardrobe.|
|May 17, 2021|Re-enabled Wardrobe.|
|Mar 8, 2023|0.18.1|Fixed Wardrobe not being unlocked at the right SkyBlock Level ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|
|Jun 26, 2023|Added an optional page argument to /wardrobe.|
|Nov 20, 2025|Added 9 Wardrobe slots obtained from [[Community Center]] upgrades.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added 2 free Wardrobe slots to 1Default ([[Ranks]]) rank, and 1 slot to 2[VIP] ([[Ranks]]), 3[VIP+] ([[Ranks]]) and 4[MVP] ([[Ranks]]) ranks.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added Wardrobe to [[Loadouts]].|
|Oct 15, 2026|Fixed the Wardrobe being unlocked for players under SkyBlock Level 5 ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|

# Wither Essence
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Wither Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Catacombs]].
## Obtaining
[[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence can be be obtained in the [[Dungeon Hub]] and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]]
- [[Dungeon Secrets]]
- [[Dungeon Hub Race]] reward from [[Guildford]].
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence.
### Increasing Wither Essence Gain
There are several sources that can be used to increase Wither Essence gained:
|Icon|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wither Shard]]|[[Wither Shard]] Essence of Wither ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1-10% more [[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence.|
|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]]|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]] Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Buffs Essence of Wither ([[Attributes]]) by +2-20%.|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|[[Tiamat Shard]] Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Buffs Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]]) by +5-50%.|
## Usage
### Star Upgrades
[[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence can be used to upgrade certain items through [[Star Upgrades]].
### Essence Shop
[[Wither Essence]] Wither Essence can be used in the Wither Essence Shop by talking to [[Malik]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] or [[Dungeon Hub]].

# Year of the Pig
Aliases: Pig Year, Pig year, YotP
Categories: Pages with UIs, Year of the Pig
Year of the Pig
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Year of the Pig is an [[Events|Event]] that happens every 12 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Years]].
## Mechanics
During the Year of the Pig, [[Piggy]] appears in the [[Village]] and [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] spawn around the [[Village]], [[Farm]], and [[Combat Settlement]].
## Shiny Pigs
[[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]] can be used on [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] to obtain rewards.
## Shop
Year of the Pig area near the [[Museum]].
[[Piggy]] sells items in exchange for [[Shiny Token|Shiny Tokens]] and other items.

|Sep 30, 2026|(Experimental) Added Pities ([[Pity and RNG Meters]]) to the [[Blood God Crest]] (1,000 Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])) and to the [[Potato Talisman]] (2,000 Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])), both affected by the effect of the [[Pig Brain Mixin]].|

# Pet Items
Aliases: Epic Combat Exp Boost, Epic Farming Exp Boost, Epic Fishing Exp Boost, Epic Foraging Exp Boost, Exp Boost, Pet Item, Pet items, Rare Combat Exp Boost, Rare Farming Exp Boost, Rare Fishing Exp Boost, Rare Mining Exp Boost, Uncommon Combat Exp Boost, Uncommon Farming Exp Boost, Uncommon Fishing Exp Boost, Uncommon Mining Exp Boost
Categories: Pet items, 0.7.8
Pet Items are items that can be given to [[Pets]] to improve them. Each pet can only hold one item at a time. To use them, equip a pet and right-click the pet while holding a pet item.
Pet items are consumed on use. Upon equipping a new item, the old item can be swapped out for the new item for a fee. An existing item can be removed with the [[Super Scrubber]], bought from [[Plumber Joe]].

## Retrieving Pet Items
Pet Items can be retrieved from [[Pets]] by applying a new Pet Item onto a Pet. Doing so refunds the previous Pet Item and costs [[Coins]] to apply and is as follows:
|Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|
A [[Super Scrubber]] can be used to remove a Pet Item without applying a new one: the cost still applies and the Super Scrubber is consumed in the process.
## Trivia
- All Pet XP boosts work multiplicatively together, such as [[Taming]], [[Diana]], and the [[Beastmaster Crest]].
- If player has multiple of the same Pet Item in their hotbar and they right-click their pet, it will always consume the item in the slot closest to the first, regardless of which slot they used on their pet.
- Crafting a [[Saddle]] grants the [[Achievements|Saddle Up!]] Achievement.
- Equipping a [[Textbook]] to a pet grants the [[Achievements|Knowledge is Power!]] Achievement.

Item ID: PET
Pet ID: BAL
Tooltip: Mining Pet; Heat Resistance: +1.5➡150; Mining Fortune: +1➡100; Furnace Grants +0.02➡2 Pristine while in the Magma Fields.; Dispersion While in the Crystal Hollows, killing mobs reduces your  Heat by 4.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Bal Pet is a [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]] obtained as a rare drop from [[Bal]].
## Obtaining
There is a 0.0126% (1 in 7,937 hits) chance to obtain an [[Bal Pet]] [[Rarity|EPIC]] Bal Pet, and a 0.005% (1 in 18,519 hits) chance for a [[Bal Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Bal Pet. The overall chance to obtain any rarity is 0.018% (1 in 5,556 hits). These chances are not affected by [[Magic Find]] or [[Pet Luck]].
Assuming Bal is fully killed and all 200 hits are from the same player, the chance for an [[Bal Pet]] [[Rarity|EPIC]] Bal Pet is 2.52% (1 in 39.68 kills), the chance for a [[Bal Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Bal Pet is 1.08% (1 in 92.59 kills), and the chance for any rarity is 3.6% (1 in 27.78 kills).

## Trivia
- The Bal Pet is inspired by the Balrog, a creature from Lord of the Rings.
- The Bal Pet can be given to [[George]] after reaching [[Skills|Taming]] 50 to increase the Taming level cap by 1.
- Multiple Bal Pets can be dropped by the same player from the same boss.
- The name Bal or Baal originates from proto-Semitic word for "owner" or "master". It got into English through Phoenician, Hebrew, Greek and Latin, where on top of the previously mentioned it gained the meaning "Lord". It is cognate with Beelzebub and Belphegor, two names for the Devil.
- While the description of Made of Lava stated it worked inside the [[Magma Fields]], it actually granted its bonus at a Y level of 64.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|1 day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|2 days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|3 days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|2.5M [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]]|5 days|

|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Frozen Blaze Skin]]|[[Frozen Blaze Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 24, 2021 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Plushie Blaze Skin]]|[[Plushie Blaze Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Summer 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Soul Blaze Skin]]|[[Soul Blaze Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Summer 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The Bling Armor ability also works with [[Frozen Blaze Armor]].
- The Fusion-Style Potato ability also works with the [[Fuming Potato Book]].
- The Fusion-Style Potato ability and Bling Armor ability are multiplicative buffs to the effects of [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]]/[[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]] on [[Blaze Armor]]/[[Frozen Blaze Armor]] which are doubled and then multiplied by 1.4 (+40%) if pet is level 100, making each [[Hot Potato Book]]/[[Fuming Potato Book]] 2.8x as effective.
- The Nether Embodiment ability also works in [[Kuudra]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 21, 2024|Nether Embodiment no longer affects [[Trophy Chance]].|
|Aug 28, 2025|Changed Nether Embodiment to only increase Combat and Miscellaneous stats.|
|Jul 3, 2025|Added COMMON ([[Rarity]]), UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) and RARE ([[Rarity]]) Blaze Pets.|
|Jul 3, 2025|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) and LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Blaze Pets are now only obtainable via [[Kat]], rather than crafting.|
|Jul 3, 2025|Changed the recipe of Blaze Pet from 1 [[Super Enchanted Egg]] and 8 [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] to 1 [[Super Enchanted Egg]] and 64 (1 stack) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]].|

|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Added COMMON ([[Rarity]]), UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) and RARE ([[Rarity]]) Blaze Pets.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) and LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Blaze Pets are now only obtainable via [[Kat]], rather than crafting.|

# Blue Whale Pet
Aliases: Blue Whale, Whale, Whale Pet, Whale pet
Categories: Pets, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Templates with deprecated argument names, Events, Traveling Zoo, Fishing pets, Fishing
[Blue Whale Pet]
Type: [[Fishing]] Pet ([[Pets]])

Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: [[Traveling Zoo]]

Buy: 25,000 [[Coins]] 1x [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] Coin + Bazaar Material Cost:

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: BLUE_WHALE
Tooltip: Fishing Pet; Health: +2➡200; Ingest If you have any absorption active, gain +0.05➡5% Damage Reduction.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Blue Whale is a [[Fishing]] Pet that can be bought from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. It focuses on [[Health]] and is one of the best pets for [[Effective Health|tanking]]. The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant of this pet provides a large amount of [[Health]], which is useful against [[Skeleton Master|Skeleton Masters]] in The [[Catacombs]] [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] because they deal massive amounts of [[True Damage]].
## Obtaining
The Blue Whale Pet can be purchased from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. Its price is dependent on its [[Rarity]].
|Rarity|Price|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Coins]], 64 (1 stack) [[Raw Cod]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]], 1 [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]], 16 [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]], 1 [[Enchanted Cooked Cod]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10,000,000 [[Coins]], 8 [[Enchanted Cooked Cod]]|

## Tips
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant of the Blue Whale pet is very good for the [[Trials of Fire]] when going for level 25 of the [[Campfire Badge|accessory]].
## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|15k [[Coins]]||1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|75k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|900k [[Coins]]|1x [[Enchanted Cooked Cod]]|7 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|9M [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Cooked Cod]]|12 Days|

## Trivia
- On initial release (26 February 2020), it used to make players gain 1 [[Defense]] for every 0.3 [[Health]] points instead of 1 for every 30, letting people that were using [[Mastiff Armor]] have more than 90,000 [[Defense]] (99.9% damage reduction), making them nearly invincible, with [[Effective Health]] over 1000x what's normally possible. Players with [[Strong Dragon Armor|Dragon Armors]] at the time could also easily exceed 5,000 [[Defense]]. Its Archimedes ability used to still pair well with the [[Mastiff Armor]], which gave tons of [[Health]] until 0.26.1.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 10, 2026|Changed Ingest to grant [[Damage]] reduction if the player has [[Absorption]], instead of granting extra +50/100/150/200/250 [[Health]] from all [[Potions]] (at max level).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Blue Whale Pet.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Reduced the [[Defense]] bonus given by Bulk from 3 [[Defense]] to 1 [[Defense]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Ingest to grant [[Damage]] reduction if the player has [[Absorption]], instead of granting extra +50/100/150/200/250 [[Health]] from all [[Potions]] (at max level).|

# Chicken Pet
Aliases: Mystery Chicken Pet
Categories: Pets, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Raw Chicken Collection, Farming pets
[Chicken Pet]

Collection ([[Collections]]): Raw Chicken III (3) ([[Raw Chicken]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[Raw Chicken]] 1x [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: CHICKEN
Tooltip: Farming Pet; Speed: +0.5➡50; Farming Fortune: +0.5➡50; Free Range Grants +0.5➡50 Farming Fortune while on Public Islands.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Chicken Pet is a craftable [[Farming]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Chicken III (3) ([[Raw Chicken]])|1x [[Egg]] 64x [[Raw Chicken]]|Recipe: [[Raw Chicken]] x8 ×8 + [[Egg]] → [Lvl 1➡100] Chicken|
### Quests
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Chicken Pet can be obtained once by presenting the [[Farmhand]] with a [[Wheat Minion|Wheat Minion I]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|500 [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Raw Chicken]] 128x [[Feather]]|10 Seconds|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Raw Chicken]] 256x [[Feather]]|20 Seconds|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Raw Chicken]] 512x [[Feather]]|30 Seconds|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Raw Chicken]] 8x [[Enchanted Feather]]|45 Seconds|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Moonflower]]|1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|4x [[Compacted Moonflower]] 2x [[Veilshroom]]|2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5M [[Coins]]|8x [[Compacted Moonflower]] 4x [[Duskbloom]]|3 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10M [[Coins]]|16x [[Compacted Moonflower]] 8x [[Do-not-eat-shroom]]|5 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Snow Crow Skin]]|[[Snow Crow Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 20, 2025 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Flower Crow Skin]]|[[Flower Crow Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Spring 2026 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin will allow you to swap between 12 floral crows!|
|[[Flower Crow Skin]]|[[Flower Crow Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)||
## Tips
- The Quick Hands perk does not apply on [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]'s Reduced to Atoms and Kinetic abilities.
- The Quick Hands perk does not apply on [[Lesser Orb of Healing]] [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
- The Insightful perk does not apply on [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]].
- Not consuming [[Mana]] due to the Insightful perk does not trigger [[Refrigerate]].
## Trivia
- If a player is morphed into a [[Rat Pet]] and moves near a player with a Crow Pet equipped, the [[Rat Pet]] is despawned and the following message is sent in chat:
  OOPS! Your Crow ate <player>'s Rat!

# Dolphin Pet
Aliases: Dolphin, Dolphin (Pet)
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, 0.7.8, Fishing pets
[Dolphin Pet]
Type: Fishing Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: DOLPHIN
Tooltip: Fishing Pet; Intelligence: +1➡100; Sea Creature Chance: +0.05➡5%; Pod Tactics Grants +0.06➡6 Fishing Speed for each player within 30; blocks, up to 5 players.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Dolphin Pet is a [[Fishing]] [[Pets|Pet]] that can be obtained directly from [[Fishing]]. Unlike other [[Fishing]] Pets, the Dolphin is obtained via milestones, similar to the [[Rock Pet]]. Upon reaching a milestone a new dolphin pet is acquired, any previous dolphins are not upgraded.
## Obtaining
### Fishing Milestones
The Dolphin Pet is obtained by fishing up [[Sea Creatures]]. Progress is granted to the player who fished up the Sea Creature when it dies, even if it is killed by another player. Sea Creatures killed before the Pets v2 update do not count towards Dolphin Pet progress.
The milestones are as follows:
|Rarity|Sea Creatures|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|250|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|1,000|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|2,500|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5,000|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10,000|
Players can see their progress to milestones in the [[Fishing]] skill menu after unlocking the first milestone.
### Shop Purchase
If lost, the player can repurchase the highest rarity they have unlocked from [[Rusty]].

|[[Red Snubfin Dolphin Skin]]|[[Red Snubfin Dolphin Skin]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - January 19, 2022 - 5,000 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant is generally not considered worthwhile to upgrade to, as the only additional perk not present in [[Rarity|EPIC]] is not too useful.
- Only [[Sea Creatures]] fished up by the player count towards the Dolphin Pet milestone, any other [[Sea Creatures]], such as from another player or naturally spawned ones, won't count. The player does not need to kill the [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] themselves, so long as the [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] is killed it will count towards their progress.
## History

|Sep 6, 2022|Pod Tactics now grants [[Fishing Speed]] for players within 30 blocks, and gives up to +10 [[Fishing Speed]] for EPIC ([[Rarity]]) and LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) rarities.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Added Dolphin Pet to [[Rusty]].|

## Obtaining
Eagle Pet can be purchased and upgraded from the [[Essence Shops|Safari Essence Shop]]:

Unlike other pets, Eagle Pet only works in [[Critter Safari]], starts at Level 100, cannot use [[Pet Items]] and grants no [[Pets|Pet Score]].

The Ender Dragon Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] obtained very rarely from killing [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Ender Dragon Pet is a drop from [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]] and has a chance to drop if the player has at least 449 ([[Rarity|EPIC]]) or 450 ([[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]) [[Ender Dragon|quality]]. This is affected by the number of [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] placed, each increasing the chances of dropping an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Ender Dragon Pet by 0.05%, and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Ender Dragon Pet by 0.01%.

## Tips
- The Ender Dragon Pet's End Strike perk and added survivability from Superior is useful when fighting the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]. For players with low [[Effective Health]], this pet provides a good mix of survivability and damage.
  - Outside of [[The End]], however, there are often cheaper alternatives that serves a similar purpose. Namely, the [[Blaze Pet]] is a strictly better option in the [[Crimson Isle]] for both survivability and damage, and the [[Ankylosaurus Pet]] usually gives more damage everywhere. Due to the hefty cost of this pet, it is often better to spend the coins on [[Attributes]] or [[Accessories]] rather than on this pet.
  - The [[Golden Dragon Pet]], despite its hefty cost and the bank requirement, is a more expensive option that is far better and more universally useful.
- In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode Floor 7]], this pet is optimal for phase 5 as the dragons are affected by End Strike.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- On Ironman, always level the pet to level 100 before upgrading to [[Rarity|Legendary]] as the coin cost is not negligible. For normal profiles, it is better to sell the [[Rarity|EPIC]] pet and buy a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] instead of upgrading at [[Kat]], or use the coins to buy pets and other upgrades that will be more generally useful.
## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|400M [[Coins]]|8x [[Summoning Eye]]|20 Days|
## Trivia
- During the Primal Dragons alpha, the Ender Dragon Pet could be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] with a [[Primal Dragon Egg]] through [[Kat]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. This granted the Ancestral ability, which grants +2 [[Strength]] for every unique pet owned.
- The Ender Dragon Pet shares a texture with the NPC that represents the Dragon Wars in the Arcade lobby.
## History

|Aug 28, 2025|Reduced required Dragon Weight from 450 to 449.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Ender Dragon Pet.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Renowned ([[Reforging]]), Superior, Superior Blood and [[Legion]] are now additive with each other rather than multiplying with each other.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|End Strike now gives an additive 2% [[Damage]] per level (from a multiplicative +0.25% per level).|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Reduced required Dragon Weight from 450 to 449.|

The Giraffe Pet can be purchased from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. Its price is dependent on its [[Rarity]].

- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.

## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|RARE]] ability, Higher Ground, is likely a reference to the famous quote from Star Wars: Revenge of the Sith when Anakin Skywalker and Obi-Wan Kenobi duel each other.
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant's ability comes from a former ability of the [[Ocelot Pet]], Evasion. It has been renamed, buffed, and moved onto this pet.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Giraffe Pet.|
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Changed Long Neck to become a combat perk. Buffed Higher Ground values|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Giraffe Pet was reworked. Added [[Swing Range]] to base stats. Higher Ground changed to grant [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] per [[Swing Range]]. Long Neck changed to deal more damage against enemies more than 3 blocks away.|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Glacite]]|1 day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Glacite]]|2 days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Enchanted Glacite]]|3 days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|2.5M [[Coins]]|8x [[Glacite Amalgamation]]|5 days|

## Trivia
- The Glacite Golem Pet and [[Bal Pet]] are the only pets that grant [[Pristine]], and both have it limited to certain areas.
- The ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] boost from Powder-powered works on [[Commissions]], killing [[Glacite Maniacs]], looting [[Frozen Corpses]], the [[Heart of the Mountain|Daily Powder HotM perk]], and the [[Heart of the Mountain|Daily Grind HotM perk]].
  - It does not affect the ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] gained from exiting a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]].

# Golem Pet
Categories: Pets, Confirmations needed, Templates with deprecated argument names, Combat pets, 0.7.8, Mob Drops

Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: EPIC ([[Rarity]]), LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: 5Rare ([[Drop Chance]])
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: Rare drop from [[End Stone Protector]]
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: GOLEM
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Health: +1.5➡150; Strength: +0.5➡50; Swing Range: +0.01➡1; Last Stand While at less than 20% HP, reduce incoming damage by 20%. Additionally, gain a temporary shield equal to 40%; of your maximum health and deal 40%; more damage.; (Lasts 12s, 60s cooldown); Ricochet Your iron plating causes 0.25➡25% of attacks to ricochet and hit the attacker.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Golem Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] dropped by the [[End Stone Protector]].
## Obtaining
The Golem Pet is a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from the [[End Stone Protector]], a [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] in the [[Dragon's Nest]]. In order to receive the pet, the player must place top five in damage when the boss is killed. Contributing 1 zealot gets ?% chance boost to get the Pet, capping at 100 kills. The current drop chance for an [[Rarity|EPIC]] variant is 2%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] after 100 [[Zealot|Zealots]] contributed, and the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] is 0.6% after 100 [[Zealot|Zealots]] contributed.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10M [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|20 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[End Golem Skin]]|[[End Golem Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - April 20, 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Clay Golem Skin]]|[[Clay Golem Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Taylor's Collection ([[Taylor]]) - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug involving the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant's Toss ability multiplying damage by 200 instead of 2.
- There used to be another bug involving the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant's Toss ability that allowed players to kill each other anywhere.
- There is an Ender Dragon fight pre-fire strategy involving the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Golem Pet where if the player shoots a couple of arrows into the Dragon Egg, then kills 4 mobs, the 5th proc will trigger the Golem Pet's Toss ability to all the arrows fired, dealing massive damage upon the Dragon spawning.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Golem Pet.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Last Stand now activates at 25% [[Health]] (instead of at 15%).|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Golem Pet was reworked. Added [[Swing Range]] to base stats. Last Stand changed to grant absorption, damage reduction and more damage. Toss changed to 5x damage at all levels.|

# Grandma Wolf Pet
Categories: Pets, Combat pets
[Grandma Wolf Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: [[Grandma Wolf]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: GRANDMA_WOLF
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Strength: +0.25➡25; Health: +1➡100; Kill Combo Gain buffs for combo kills.; Effects stack as you increase your combo.; 5 Combo (lasts 8.02➡10s); +1%  Magic Find 10 Combo (lasts 6.02➡8s); +2 coins per kill 15 Combo (lasts 4.02➡6s); +1%  Magic Find 20 Combo (lasts 3.02➡5s); +5☯ Combat Wisdom 25 Combo (lasts 3.01➡4s); +1%  Magic Find 30 Combo (lasts 2.01➡3s); +2 coins per kill; This pet's perks are active even when the pet is not summoned!; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Grandma Wolf Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] that grants various perks for combo kills, including [[Magic Find]], more [[Combat]] XP, and more [[Coins]] per monster kill. It does not need to be active in order to give the combo perks.
## Obtaining
The [[Rarity|COMMON]] pet is obtained by talking to [[Grandma Wolf]]. If the pet is somehow lost, a replacement can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
### Rarity
This pet cannot be upgraded through [[Kat]]. Instead, its [[Rarity]] is upgraded by reaching certain [[Bestiary]] Milestones and talking to [[Grandma Wolf]]. The [[Bestiary]] Milestones are as follows:
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]: [[Bestiary]] Milestone V (5)
- [[Rarity|RARE]]: [[Bestiary]] Milestone X (10)
- [[Rarity|EPIC]]: [[Bestiary]] Milestone XV (15)
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]: [[Bestiary]] Milestone XX (20)

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Real Grandma Wolf Skin]]|[[Real Grandma Wolf Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - May 18, 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- While the Grandma Wolf Pet does not need to be equipped in order to grant the perks from combo kills, it is still recommended to level it as the duration of combos increases with level. Therefore, an [[Pet Items|EXP Share]] is the recommended [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] for this pet.
- Since the Grandma Wolf Pet is both free to claim and its rarity is relatively easy to level up, it is incredibly useful for gaining [[Pets|Pet Score]].
## Trivia
- The [[Grandma Wolf Pet]] Grandma Wolf Pet, [[Spirit Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Parrot Pet]], [[Owl Pet]] and [[Bingo Pet]] are the only pets that grant perks to the player even when they are not equipped.
- The Grandma Wolf Pet's Kill Combo ability can be upgraded through the [[Attributes|Combo]] attribute, obtained from syphoning [[Soul of the Alpha Shard|Soul of the Alpha Shards]], granting +0.1-1% [[Damage]] for every combo up to 50 combos.
- The Grandma Wolf Pet can have the [[Grandma's Knitting Needle]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] applied to it. It grants +5% [[Magic Find]] with a kill combo of 50 or above.
- The Grandma Wolf Pet's appearance is a reference to the fairy tale Little Red Riding Hood, where a wolf eats the protagonist's grandmother and dresses as her.
- The effects of Kill Combo can continue above the highest beneficial count, scaling upwards presumably infinitely.
- The Kill Combo perk does not work on [[Private Island|Private Islands]].
- The [[Magic Find]] bonus from the Grandma Wolf Pet is an additive percentage bonus rather than a flat bonus.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|May 11, 2022|Added Grandma Wolf Pet to [[Rusty]].|

## Notes
- [[Zombuddy]] and [[Timestalk Clone]] don't count as [[Pests]] for the Hunter's Insight ability, leading to the maximum being 157.5 [[Farming Fortune]] as of SkyBlock version 0.24.4.
## Trivia
- A [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] provides the same [[Farming Fortune]] against [[Pests]] as a [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Hedgehog Pet at 4,214.3 [[Strength]].
  - This is not feasible, as sacrificing other sources of [[Farming Fortune]] results in a net negative, and reaching that amount of [[Strength]] outside of dungeons is generally impossible.
- A [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Hedgehog Pet provides 0.5 [[Farming Fortune]] more than a [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Elephant Pet]] at 215 [[Bestiary|Pest Bestiary]] Tiers, and 7.5 more at 225 [[Bestiary|Pest Bestiary]] Tiers (max).
  - [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Elephant Pet]] is always better than a Hedgehog Pet by at least 42.5 [[Farming Fortune]] , due to its higher base stats.
- Against Pests, A [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Hedgehog Pet is better by 107.5 [[Farming Fortune]] at the maximum 225 [[Bestiary|Pest Bestiary]] tiers, while also granting an additional 35 [[Overbloom]].
- Against Pests, A [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Hedgehog Pet is the best alternative to the [[Rose Dragon Pet]], only being worse by 39.2 [[Farming Fortune]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|500 [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Leather]]|10 Seconds|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Leather]]|20 Seconds|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Leather]]|30 Seconds|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Leather]]|45 Seconds|
## Trivia
- A [[Saddle]] used to be only equippable by a Horse Pet or [[Skeleton Horse Pet]].
- Prior to being changed to a head, a Horse Pet could be used to glitch through walls.
- The movement of a Horse Pet was made server-side, which could create input lag with a higher ping.
- Prior to 0.24.5, the Horse Pet was the only pet in the game which was a direct downgrade from another pet, being the [[Skeleton Horse Pet]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 3, 2025|Adjusted Horse Pet crafting recipes and [[Kat]] costs.|
|Apr 30, 2026|Horse Pet is no longer a mount.|
|Apr 30, 2026|Replaced Rideable with High Stride, which grants permanent Jump Boost IV.|
|Apr 30, 2026|Replaced Run with Stampede: mob kills grant a stack of +1 [[Speed]] and +1-10 [[Strength]] for 5s (max 20 stacks).|
|Apr 30, 2026|Replaced Ride Into Battle with Trample: after falling 20 or more blocks, absorb your fall damage and deal 0.5-5% of your weapon's [[Damage]] for every block fallen to all mobs within 3 blocks.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Horse Pet.|
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Ride into Battle perk.|
|Feb 28, 2020|Players can no longer ride another player's Horse Pet.|
|Mar 24, 2023|Fixed a typo in the Horse Pet's lore.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Adjusted Horse Pet crafting recipes and [[Kat]] costs.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Horse Pet is no longer a mount.|

# Hound Pet
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Confirmations needed, Combat pets, 0.7.8, Mob Drops
[Hound Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: EPIC ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: Rare drop from Wolves ([[Wolf]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: HOUND
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Strength: +0.4➡40; Attack Speed: +0.25➡25%; Ferocity: +0.05➡5; Speed: +0.1➡10; Scavenger Gain +1➡100 coins per monster kill.; Finder Increases the chance for monsters to drop their armor by 0.3➡30%.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Hound Pet is a [[Combat]] Pet. It is designed for [[Wolf Slayer]].
## Obtaining
The Hound Pet can be obtained from killing [[Wolf|Wolves]] in the [[Ruins]] or [[The Park]].
|Mob|Drop Chance EPIC|Drop Chance LEGENDARY|
|-|-|-|
|[Lv15] [[Wolf]]|0.001%; (1 in 100,000)|0.0003%; (1 in 333,333.33)|

## Bugs
- Some players have reported that the Hound Pet's Finder ability does not actually boost drop chances.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Tips
- Despite the Hound Pet no longer granting a multiplicative [[Combat|Combat XP]] buff against Wolves, it still grants more additive [[Combat Wisdom]] than the [[Wolf Pet]] against Wolves.
- Assuming a weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] is used, and the player has over 200 [[Magic Find]] without a pet, the Hound Pet beats a [[Golden Dragon Pet]] with 100,000,000 (1,562,500 stacks) [[Gold Ingot]] collection and a [[Hephaestus Relic]] for armor drops.
  - However, care should be taken, as if the modifiers used (e.g. [[Looting]], [[Luck]], and the Hound Pet) push the item's base drop chance to 5% or above, [[Magic Find]] will no longer apply to the drop.
## Trivia
- The Hound Pet is one of 5 pets designed for its respective [[Slayer]]: the others being the [[Ghoul Pet]] for [[Zombie Slayer]], the [[Tarantula Pet]] for [[Spider Slayer]], the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]] for [[Enderman Slayer]], and the [[Wisp Pet]] for [[Blaze Slayer]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Buffed Scavenger from 0.05 [[Coins]] to 1 Coin ([[Coins]]) per level.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Pack Slayer now grants +50 [[Combat Wisdom]] instead of 1.5x Combat XP.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Hound Pet.|
|Oct 20, 2020|0.9.8|The Hound Pet now gives [[Ferocity]], alongside other stats.|
|Nov 8, 2021|The Finder ability no longer provides double drops to most head items (e.g. Sludge Juice).|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Hound Pet was reworked. Added more [[Attack Speed]] to base stats. Fury Claws replaced with Pack Slayer, now grants more Combat XP ([[Combat]]) from Wolves ([[Wolf]]) instead.|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Buffed Scavenger from 0.05 [[Coins]] to 1 Coin ([[Coins]]) per level.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Pack Slayer now grants +50 [[Combat Wisdom]] instead of 1.5x Combat XP.|

# Jellyfish Pet
Aliases: Jellyfish
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Dark Auction, Alchemy, 0.7.8, Alchemy pets

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: JELLYFISH
Tooltip: Alchemy Pet; Health: +2➡200; Health Regen: +1➡100; Radiant Scyphozoa While in dungeons, reduces the mana cost of Power Orbs by 0.5➡50%.; Stored Energy While in dungeons, for every 2,000 HP you heal teammates the cooldown of Wish is reduced by 0.01➡1s, up to 30s.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Jellyfish Pet is an [[Alchemy]] Pet that can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]]. Similar to all other Dark Auction pets, there are only two rarities of this pet, [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]. It is geared toward the [[Healer]] class for [[Dungeons]], because of its multiple abilities which buff [[Healing]].
## Obtaining
### Dark Auction
The Jellyfish Pet has a chance to appear in round 1, 3 or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]] with starting bid of 10,000,000 [[Coins]]. The [[Rarity|EPIC]] has an overall 22.706% chance to appear, and the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] has a 6.05% chance to appear.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|15M [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Slimeball]]|10 days|

## Tips
- A [[Bubblegum]] is recommended on this pet. It can double the duration of [[Power Orbs]], which is useful in boss fights. When using an [[Overflux Power Orb]] or [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]], its duration can be extended to 2 minutes.

The Kuudra Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] obtained from the [[Kuudra]] [[Collections|Collection]].
## Obtaining
The [[Rarity|COMMON]] Kuudra Pet appears in the Kuudra Collection after reaching [[Kuudra|Kuudra I (1)]].
Higher rarities of the Kuudra Pet can be upgraded with [[Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra Chunks]], which can be obtained from the [[Kuudra|Kuudra Collection]].

## Trivia
- The Kuudra Pet, along with the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Spirit Pet]], [[Parrot Pet]], [[Owl Pet]] and the [[Bingo Pet]] are the only pets in the game who grants perks even when unequipped.
- The Kuudra Pet used to be one of few pets that couldn't be removed from pet menu once obtained.
  - This was later changed because obtaining multiple Kuudra Pets would slow pet leveling due to how they work
- The Kuudra Pet is the only [[Pets|Pet]] that can gain Pet Experience without being equipped in an Exp Share Slot.
  - The Kuudra Pet cannot equip an [[Exp Share]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. Trying to equip one grants the message: This item is not compatible with your pet!
  - Other pet exp boost items like [[Pet Items|Combat Exp Boost]] will not increase experience gain when the Kuudra Pet is not equipped.
  - If the Kuudra Pet is equipped in an Exp Share Slot, it will gain shared exp twice.
- The Kuudra Pet gives the second most flat [[Health]] out of all [[Pets]] in the game after the [[Wisp Pet|Subzero Wisp Pet]].
  - The Kuudra Pet grants up to 400 [[Health]] at Level 100 while a [[Wisp Pet|Subzero Wisp Pet]] grants 600 [[Health]].
  - A [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blue Whale Pet]] can still give more as it adds 20% [[Health]].
- The Kuudra Pet is one of 4 pets that cannot be upgraded by [[Kat]], alongside the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Wisp Pet]], and [[Bingo Pet]].
- Before 0.17.1, a player could obtain multiple Kuudra Pets if they already had one with the same or higher [[Rarity]].
  - It is unknown if this was intentional.

## Obtaining
The Lion Pet can be purchased from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. Its price is dependent on its [[Rarity]].

## Usage
This pet gives quite a lot of a bonus to [[First Strike]], [[Triple-Strike]] and, [[Combo]], making it ideal to one-hit certain mobs, such as [[Treasure Hoarder|Treasure Hoarders]].
## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|15k [[Coins]]||1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|75k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|900k [[Coins]]||7 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|14M [[Coins]]|1,024x [[Enchanted Raw Beef]]|12 Days|

## Trivia
- The bonus damage from the King of the Jungle perk is part of the [[Damage Calculation|additive damage multiplier]], hence it is suppressed by other factors, such as [[Combat|Combat level]] and [[Enchantments]]. This means that the actual damage increase from this perk is much lower.
- The damage from the Primal Force perk is multiplicative with other factors, such as the [[One For All]] Enchantment and [[Catacombs|Catacombs level]].

Item ID: PET
Pet ID: MONKEY
Tooltip: Foraging Pet; Intelligence: +0.5➡50; Speed: +0.2➡20; Treeborn Grants +0.4➡40  Foraging Fortune, which increases your chance at double logs.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Monkey is a [[Foraging]] Pet that can be bought from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] Event. It grants [[Speed]] and [[Intelligence]], as well as abilities specifically for increasing your [[Foraging]] efficiency.
## Obtaining
The Monkey Pet can be purchased from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. Its price is dependent on its [[Rarity]].
|Rarity|Price|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Coins]], 64 (1 stack) [[Jungle Log]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]], 1 [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]], 16 [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]], 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|18,000,000 [[Coins]], 512 (8 stacks) [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Time|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|15k [[Coins]]|1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|75k [[Coins]]|2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|900k [[Coins]]|7 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|17M [[Coins]]|12 Days|

## Tips
- For [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players who don't mind waiting or using [[Kat Flower|Kat Flowers]], it is recommended to buy a [[Rarity|COMMON]]–[[Rarity|RARE]] Monkey Pet, as upgrading it via [[Kat]] allows you to skip grinding large amounts of wood.
## Trivia
- After the Pets V2 update came out, the first few Traveling Zoo events only featured [[Rarity|COMMON]] Monkey Pets.

# Montezuma Pet
Aliases: Montezuma
Categories: Pets
[Montezuma Pet]
Type: Fractured Soul Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: RARE ([[Rarity]])–EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: MONTEZUMA
Tooltip: Fractured Soul Pet; Rift Time: +25; Nine Lives Gain +15 Rift Time per soul piece.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; RARE
The Montezuma Pet is a [[Pets|Pet]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]. The player can upgrade it by finding Montezuma Soul Pieces around the Rift.
## Obtaining
A [[Rarity|RARE]] Montezuma Pet can be obtained from [[Sad Jacquelle]] in the [[Wyld Woods]] after starting their quest and finding the first Montezuma Soul Piece. It is [Lv100] by default.
## Game Message
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interacting with soul before talking to [[Sad Jacquelle]]|Looks like a floating cat skull... Average rift find to be honest.|
|Interacting with soul after talking to [[Sad Jacquelle]]|You feel a powerful cat-like energy rushing to your body... You found Montezuma, or whatever is left of it... I should report back to Jacquelle...|
## Montezuma Soul Pieces
There are 9 Montezuma Soul Pieces that can be found in the Rift. After finding 6 pieces, the Montezuma Pet is upgraded to [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Rarity]]. Each piece found adds +15 [[Rift Time]] to the Pet, and +2 [[Mana Regen]] when it is [[Rarity|EPIC]].
Note: Click on the rows to keep track of the Montezuma Souls collected. This persists even after reloading or refreshing the page.:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Area|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|-93.5|69|112.5|Underneath a tree in the [[Wyld Woods]]|
|2|0|56|63.5|In a house in the [[Rift Gallery]], which can be accessed by pressing the button next to its door|
|3|-139.7|45|15|Behind the waterfall in [[Leeches Lair]]|
|4|-62.5|88|-81.5|On top of the house containing [[Cosmo]], [[Finplex]], and [[Plumber Joe]] in the [[West Village]]|
|5|-24.5|72|-198.5|Behind a wall in [[Dreadfarm]] (requires [[Horsezooka]] to break)|
|6|2.5|63|-16.5|Accessible by clicking the Bookshelf at 8, 72, -15 ([[Coordinates]]) in the flower shop on the [[Village Plaza]]|
|7|38.5|65|-117.5|On a floating island east of [[Déjà Vu Alley]]|
|8|22.5|43|-219.5|Underneath a snow block in the [[Living Stillness]], which can be broken|
|9|152.5|80|-13.5|In the house's second floor at the end of the [[Photon Pathway]]|
## Stats
[[Rarity|RARE]] Montezuma Pet
- Max Stats (Level 100) ⓘ (Stats of a maxed-out pet)VED
- Stats: [[Rift Time]]: +25 Abilities: Nine Lives – Gain +15 [[Rift Time]] per soul piece.
[[Rarity|EPIC]] Montezuma Pet
- Max Stats (Level 100) ⓘVED
- Stats: [[Rift Time]]: +25 [[Mana Regen]]: +0 Abilities: Nine Lives – Gain +15 [[Rift Time]] per soul piece. Trickery – Gain +2 [[Mana Regen]] per soul piece found.
## Trivia
- The Montezuma Pet gives a base 10 [[Rift Time]], but the minimum amount the player can get from it is 25 [[Rift Time]], due to the Nine Lives ability and needing at least one soul piece to obtain the pet.
- The Montezuma Pet cannot be transferred out of the Rift and does not contribute to the player's [[Pets|Pet Score]].
- If the player somehow manages to get a [[Tier Boost]] into the Rift, it is possible to apply it to the Montezuma Pet, and it will become [Lv96] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]. It does not gain any additional stats or perks.
  - The SkyBlock Menu still shows the pet as [Lv100] [[Rarity|EPIC]], and it will be reverted to such when the player leaves and rejoins the Rift.
- The Montezuma Pet appears to be a reference to the book The Nine Lives of Montezuma, which is about a cat named Montezuma, hence the Montezuma Pet also being a cat.
